*        select single
*          *
*          from zgnfa_approval
*          into @data(ls_APPROVAL)
*          where nfatype = @ls_dnfa_head-nfa_type
*          and gencat = @ls_dnfa_head-gen_category
*          and ekgrp = @ls_dnfa_head-purchase_grp
*          and nfaamt = @lv_amt
*          and opex_capex = @ls_dnfa_head-opex_capex.

        select
          *
          from zgnfa_approval
          into @data(ls_APPROVAL) up to 1 rows
          where nfatype     = @ls_dnfa_head-nfa_type
            and gencat      = @ls_dnfa_head-gen_category
            and ekgrp       = @ls_dnfa_head-purchase_grp
            and opex_capex  = @ls_dnfa_head-opex_capex
            and amount_from <= @ls_dnfa_head-contract_val
            and amount_to   >= @ls_dnfa_head-contract_val
          order by primary key.
        endselect.

            and amount_from <= @ls_dnfa_head-contract_val
            and amount_to   >= @ls_dnfa_head-contract_val
          order by primary key.
        endselect.

        if lv_len > 255.
          read table lt_query into data(wa_query1) with key
        nfaid = lt_data-nfaid question = <query>-question+0(255)
        question1 = <query>-question+255.
          if sy-subrc = 0.
            if lv_len1 > 255.
              update zgnfa_query_tab
            set answer = @<query>-answer+0(255),
             answer1 = @<query>-answer+255
            where nfaid = @lt_data-nfaid
              and question = @wa_query1-question
              and question1 = @wa_query1-question1
              and counter = @wa_query1-counter.
            else.
              update zgnfa_query_tab
            set answer = @<query>-answer
            where nfaid = @lt_data-nfaid
              and question = @wa_query1-question
              and question1 = @wa_query1-question1
              and counter = @wa_query1-counter.
            endif.
          else.
            qcounter  = qcounter + 1.
            wa_query-nfaid  = LT_dATA-nfaid.
            wa_query-question = <query>-question+0(255).
            wa_query-question1 = <query>-question+255.
            wa_query-counter  = qcounter.
            if lv_len1 > 255.
              wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer+0(255).
              wa_query-answer1  = <query>-answer+255.
            else.
              wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer.
            endif.
            wa_query-querychk = 'X'.
            if <query>-raisedby is initial.
              select single name from zrfq_user_master
              into @data(raisedbyname) where id = @lv_loginid
              and usertype = 'APPROVER'.
              wa_query-raisedby  = raisedbyname.
            else.
              wa_query-raisedby = <query>-raisedby.
            endif.
            wa_query-raisedon = sy-datum.
            wa_query-crtat  = sy-uzeit.
            wa_query-crtby  = sy-uname.
            wa_query-crton  = sy-datlo .
            append wa_query to it_query.
            clear : wa_query.
          endif.
        else.
          read table lt_query into wa_query1 with key
                  nfaid = lt_data-nfaid question = <query>-question.
          if sy-subrc = 0.
            if lv_len1 > 255.
              update zgnfa_query_tab
              set answer = @<query>-answer+0(255),
                  answer1 = @<query>-answer+255
              where nfaid = @lt_data-nfaid
                and question = @wa_query1-question
                and counter = @wa_query1-counter .
            else.
              update zgnfa_query_tab
              set answer = @<query>-answer
              where nfaid = @lt_data-nfaid
                and question = @wa_query1-question
                and counter = @wa_query1-counter.
            endif.
          else.
            qcounter  = qcounter + 1.
            wa_query-nfaid  = LT_dATA-nfaid.
            wa_query-question = <query>-question.
            wa_query-counter  = qcounter.
            if lv_len1 > 255.
              wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer+0(255).
              wa_query-answer1  = <query>-answer+255.
            else.
              wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer.
            endif.
            wa_query-querychk = 'X'.
            if <query>-raisedby is initial.
              select single name from zrfq_user_master
              into @raisedbyname where id = @lv_loginid
              and usertype = 'APPROVER'.
              wa_query-raisedby  = raisedbyname.
            else.
              wa_query-raisedby = <query>-raisedby.
            endif.
            wa_query-raisedon = sy-datum.
            wa_query-crtat  = sy-uzeit.
            wa_query-crtby  = sy-uname.
            wa_query-crton  = sy-datlo .
            append wa_query to it_query.
            clear : wa_query.
          endif.
        endif.
        clear : lv_len,lv_len1.

**---------------EOC Ali/Vishweshwar on 19.06.2026 15:42:42----------------*
*        select single
*    *
*    from zgnfa_approval
*    into @ls_APPROVAL
*    where nfatype = 'DIRECT'
*    and gencat = @ls_dnfa_head-gen_category
*    and ekgrp = @ls_dnfa_head-purchase_grp
*    and nfaamt = @lv_amt
*    and opex_capex = @ls_dnfa_head-opex_capex.

        select
          *
          from zgnfa_approval
          into @ls_APPROVAL up to 1 rows
          where nfatype     = 'DIRECT'
            and gencat      = @ls_dnfa_head-gen_category
            and ekgrp       = @ls_dnfa_head-purchase_grp
            and opex_capex  = @ls_dnfa_head-opex_capex
            and amount_from <= @ls_dnfa_head-contract_val
            and amount_to   >= @ls_dnfa_head-contract_val
          order by primary key.
        endselect.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->/IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~GET_EXPANDED_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH(optional)
* | [--->] IO_EXPAND                      TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ODATA_EXPAND(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE REF TO DATA
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [<---] ET_EXPANDED_CLAUSES            TYPE        STRING_TABLE
* | [<---] ET_EXPANDED_TECH_CLAUSES       TYPE        STRING_TABLE
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /iwbep/if_mgw_appl_srv_runtime~get_expanded_entity.
    types : begin of ty_data.
              include type    zcl_zdirect_nfa_proces_mpc=>ts_headerdetails.
    types :   HeaderItemnav type table of zcl_zdirect_nfa_proces_mpc=>ts_itemdetails with default key,
              HeaderToVDDNP type table of zcl_zdirect_nfa_proces_mpc=>ts_awardedvenvdd with default key,
            end of ty_data.
    data : ls_data      type ty_data,
           ls_keys      type zcl_zdirect_nfa_proces_mpc_ext=>ts_headerdetails,
           lo_facade    type ref to /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_int_facade,
           lv_loginid   type string,
           lv_logintype type char1,
           lv_userrole  type char1,
           lv_bukrs     type bukrs,
           lv_lifnr     type lifnr,
           lv_level     type char10,
           vdd_ven      type lfa1-lifnr.
*---------------BOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*
*           lv_amt       type zde_nfaamout.
*---------------EOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*

    data : id               type  thead-tdid,
           language         type  thead-tdspras,
           name1            type  thead-tdname,
           object           type  thead-tdobject,
           LV_scope         type string,
           lv_justification type  string.

    data : lines    type table of tline,
           ls_lines like line of lines.

    lo_facade  ?= /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).

    zcl_supp_portal_utility=>read_header_parameter( exporting io_facade   = lo_facade
                                                    importing e_logintype = lv_logintype
                                                              e_loginid   = lv_loginid
                                                              e_userrole  = lv_userrole
                                                              e_ccode     = lv_bukrs ).

    io_tech_request_context->get_converted_keys(
      importing
        es_key_values = ls_keys ).

    case iv_entity_set_name.
      when 'HeaderDetailsSet'.

        id = 'ST'.
        language = 'EN'.
        name1  = |{ 'DNFAS-' && ls_keys-nfaid }|.
        condense name1.
        object = 'TEXT'.

        call function 'READ_TEXT'
          exporting
            client                  = sy-mandt
            id                      = id
            language                = language
            name                    = name1
            object                  = object
          tables
            lines                   = lines
          exceptions
            id                      = 1
            language                = 2
            name                    = 3
            not_found               = 4
            object                  = 5
            reference_check         = 6
            wrong_access_to_archive = 7
            others                  = 8.
        if sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
        endif.

        loop at lines into ls_lines.
          concatenate lv_scope ls_lines-tdline into lv_scope separated by space.
        endloop.

*lv_scope = lv_text.
        clear :  id , language , name1 , object ,ls_lines.
        refresh : lines.



        id = 'ST'.
        language = 'EN'.
        name1  = |{ 'DNFAJ-' && ls_keys-nfaid }|.
        condense name1.
        object = 'TEXT'.

        call function 'READ_TEXT'
          exporting
            client                  = sy-mandt
            id                      = id
            language                = language
            name                    = name1
            object                  = object
          tables
            lines                   = lines
          exceptions
            id                      = 1
            language                = 2
            name                    = 3
            not_found               = 4
            object                  = 5
            reference_check         = 6
            wrong_access_to_archive = 7
            others                  = 8.
        if sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
        endif.


        loop at lines into ls_lines.
          concatenate lv_justification ls_lines-tdline into lv_justification separated by space.
        endloop.

*lv_justification = lv_text.
        clear :  id , language , name1 , object ,ls_lines.
        refresh : lines.

        clear : vdd_ven.
        select single
        *

        from zdirect_nfa_head
        into @data(ls_head)
              where nfaid = @ls_keys-nfaid.

        vdd_ven = ls_head-proposed_vend.
        vdd_ven = |{ vdd_ven alpha = out }|.

        select
          *
          from zvdd_master
          where vendorcode = @vdd_ven
          into table @data(it_vdd).

        ls_DATA-nfaid           = ls_head-nfaid.
        shift ls_DATA-nfaid left deleting leading '0'.
        ls_DATA-initior         = ls_head-initiator.
        ls_DATA-purgrp          = ls_head-purchase_grp.

        select single
          *
          from zdepartment_det
          into @data(ls_department_det)
          where pur_grp = @ls_DATA-purgrp.
        if sy-subrc is initial.

*        select single
*                   *
*                   from zgnfa_approval

*                   and gencat = @ls_DATA-generalcategory

        select
          *
          from zgnfa_approval
          into @data(ls_APPROVAL) up to 1 rows
          where nfatype     = @ls_head-nfa_type
            and gencat      = @ls_head-gen_category
            and ekgrp       = @ls_head-purchase_grp
            and opex_capex  = @ls_head-opex_capex
            and amount_from <= @ls_head-contract_val
            and amount_to   >= @ls_head-contract_val
          order by primary key.
        endselect.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->/IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~GET_STREAM
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [<---] ER_STREAM                      TYPE REF TO DATA
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~GET_STREAM.
data: ls_key_tab     type /iwbep/s_mgw_name_value_pair,
      PRITMKEY TYPE DRAD-OBJKY,
      DOCID TYPE DOKNR,
      lv_file_name    type char200,
      ls_stream      type ty_s_media_resource,
            ls_header      TYPE ihttpnvp,
     lv_name_utf8    TYPE string,
        lv_filename_str TYPE string.
constants: lc_content_disp TYPE string VALUE 'Content-Disposition' ##NO_TEXT,
           lc_attach_utf8  TYPE string VALUE 'attachment; filename*=UTF-8''''' ##NO_TEXT.


CASE iv_entity_set_name.
WHEN  'GetDnfaAttachmentSet'.
read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'NFAID'.
IF sy-subrc = 0.
DATA(NFAID) = ls_key_tab-value.
ENDIF.

  read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'DOCID'.
IF sy-subrc = 0.
DOCID = ls_key_tab-value.
ENDIF.

SELECT FROM ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT
  FIELDS
       DOKAR,
       DOKNR,
       DOKVR,
       DOKTL
  WHERE NFAID = @NFAID AND DOKNR = @DOCID
  ORDER BY DOKVR DESCENDING
  INTO @DATA(LS_RFQATT) UP TO 1 ROWS.
ENDSELECT.

try.
     call method zcl_supp_portal_utility=>retrieve_file_from_dms
              exporting
                dokar     = ls_RFQatt-dokar
                doknr     = ls_RFQatt-doknr
                dokvr     = ls_RFQatt-dokvr
                doktl     = ls_RFQatt-doktl
              importing
                mimetype  = ls_stream-mime_type
                content   = ls_stream-value
                file_name = lv_file_name.
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception .
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception .
        endtry.

          ls_header-name = lc_content_disp.
          lv_filename_str = lv_file_name.
          lv_name_utf8 = cl_http_utility=>if_http_utility~escape_url( unescaped =  lv_filename_str ).
          REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '%2e' IN lv_name_utf8 WITH '.'.
          CONCATENATE lc_attach_utf8 lv_name_utf8 INTO ls_header-value.
          set_header( is_header = ls_header ).

        ENDCASE.



        copy_data_to_ref( exporting is_data = ls_stream
                    changing  cr_data = er_stream ).
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->ATTACHMENT_DDSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TT_ATTACHMENT_DD
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method ATTACHMENT_DDSET_GET_ENTITYSET.
SELECT FROM ZRFQ_DASHB_DD
  fields DISTINCT
  value_dd as value
  where key_dd = 'NFAAT'
  INTO TABLE @ET_ENTITYSET.
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->AWARDEDVENDDSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TT_AWARDEDVENVDD
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method AWARDEDVENDDSET_GET_ENTITYSET.

data : lt_ven TYPE RANGE OF zvdd_master-vendorcode,
       ls_ven like LINE OF lt_ven.
constants lc_prop_vendor type string value 'Vendor' ##NO_TEXT.
try.
        data(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = lc_prop_vendor ].
        loop at ls_filter-select_options into data(ls_selopt).
*            data(ls_selopt) = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
         ls_ven-low = |{ ls_selopt-low ALPHA = OUT }|.
          ls_ven-sign   = 'I'.
          ls_ven-option = 'EQ'.
          append  ls_ven to lt_ven.
          clear : ls_selopt.
        endloop.
        clear : ls_filter.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.


          SELECT * FROM ZVDD_MASTER WHERE
             vendorcode IN @lt_ven
*             vendorcode in @lt_ven
            INTO TABLE @DATA(IT_VDD).

et_entityset =       value #( for wa_vdd in it_vdd
            (
*      nfaid                          = ls_HEAD-nfaid
*      EventID                        = ls_data1-eventid
      Inco_Year                      = wa_vdd-inco_year
      Bussiness                      = wa_vdd-business
      KeyPers1                       = wa_vdd-keyper1
      KeyPers2                       = wa_vdd-keyper2
      AnnualRevLastFY                = wa_vdd-annualrevfy
      AnnualRevLastToLastFY          = wa_vdd-annualrevlfy
      Asso_Azure                     = wa_vdd-asso_azure
      VDD_Appli                      = wa_vdd-vddapp
      VDD_Status                     = wa_vdd-vddstatus
      pep                            = wa_vdd-pep
      vendor                  =  wa_vdd-venDOrname
      Risk_Rating                    = wa_vdd-risk_rate
      CE_App                         = wa_vdd-ce_off
      migration_f         = wa_vdd-migration_f
      reason_vdd  =  wa_vdd-reason_vdd

                                           ) ) .

  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->DNFAATTACHMENTSE_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TT_DNFAATTACHMENT
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method DNFAATTACHMENTSE_GET_ENTITYSET.
data : r_NFAID  type RANGE OF  ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT-NFAID,
           l_NFAID  like line of r_NFAID,
           er_entity like LINE OF et_entityset.

    try.
            DATA(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ].
           loop at ls_filter-select_options INTO DATA(ls_selopt).
            l_NFAid-low = ls_selopt-low.
            l_NFAid-sign   = 'I'.
            l_NFAid-option = 'EQ'.
            append  l_NFAid to r_NFAid.
            clear : ls_selopt.
            ENDLOOP.
            clear : ls_filter.
          catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
        endtry.


SELECT FROM ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT

  FIELDS
  RFQID,
  NFAID,
  FILE_NAME,
  UPLOADED_BY,
  DOKNR,
  Specification,
  file_type,
  SENDTOSUPPLIER,
  SENDTOPO
  WHERE NFAID IN @r_NFAid and attachmenttype = 'DNFA'
  INTO  TABLE @DATA(IT_DATA).

et_entityset =  VALUE #( for LS_DATA IN IT_DATA INDEX INTO IND (

                         SRNO = IND
                         Specification = ls_data-Specification
                         FileName = ls_data-FILE_NAME
                         FileType = ls_data-file_type
                         DocID  = ls_data-DOKNR
                         SendToSupplier = ls_data-SENDTOSUPPLIER
                         RFQID = ls_data-rfqid
                         SENDTOPO = LS_DATA-sendtopo
                         NFAID  = LS_DATA-nfaid

                         ) ).
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->GRPDETHELPSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TT_GRPDETHELP
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method grpdethelpset_get_entityset.
    data : lv_search_string type string,
           lt_grp_Det       type range of char20,
           ls_grp_Det       like line of lt_grp_Det.

    if iv_search_string is not initial.
      lv_search_string = iv_search_string.
      translate lv_search_string to upper case.
      concatenate '*' lv_search_string '*' into lv_search_string.

      ls_grp_Det-sign = 'I'.
      ls_grp_Det-option = 'CP'.
      translate lv_search_string to upper case.
      ls_grp_Det-low = lv_search_string.
      append ls_grp_Det to lt_grp_Det.
      clear ls_grp_Det.

    endif.

    if iv_search_string is initial.
      select grp_id,
             category,
             type,
             nfa_cond from zgroup_details into table @et_entityset where grp_id in @lt_grp_Det.
    else.
      select grp_id,
      category,
      type,
      nfa_cond from zgroup_details into table @et_entityset where ( grp_id in @lt_grp_Det or category in @lt_grp_Det
                or type in @lt_grp_Det or nfa_cond in @lt_grp_Det ).
    endif.

    sort et_entityset ascending by grpdet.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->HEADERDETAILSSET_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TS_HEADERDETAILS
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method HEADERDETAILSSET_GET_ENTITY.

 DATA: ls_key        TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_name_value_pair.

  READ TABLE it_key_tab INTO ls_key WITH KEY name = 'NDAId'.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
   data(lv_id) = ls_key-value.
  ENDIF.

  select act_buyer, purgroup from zrfq_prdashboard into @data(ls_data) up to 1 rows
    where act_buyer <> @space
    order by act_buyer, purgroup.
  endselect.

    er_entity-initior = ls_data-ACT_BUYER.
    er_entity-purgrp = ls_data-PURGROUP.
    er_entity-nfatype = text-020.
    er_entity-contractval = 'ARC'.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->HEADERDETAILSSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TT_HEADERDETAILS
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method headerdetailsset_get_entityset.
    types: begin of ty_nfa,
             nfatype     type zde_nfatype,
             gencat      type zde_gencat,
             ekgrp       type ekgrp,
             opex_capex  type zde_astcls,
             contractval type zde_conval,
*---------------BOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*
*             nfaamt      type zde_nfaamout,
*---------------EOC Ali on 03.08.2026----------------*
           end of ty_nfa.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->HISTORYSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TT_HISTORY
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method historyset_get_entityset.
*    data(lv_nfaid) = it_key_tab[ name = 'NFAID' ]-value.
    data(lv_nfaid) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    if lv_nfaid is not initial.
      select
        nfaid,
        alevel as level,
        action,
        remark,
         cast( edate as char( 8 ) ) as date,
         cast( etime as char( 8 ) ) as time
        from ZdNFA_HIST
        into table @et_entityset
        where nfaid = @lv_nfaid.
      if et_entityset is not initial.
        loop at et_entityset assigning field-symbol(<ls_entityset>).
          if <ls_entityset>-action = 'A'.
            <ls_entityset>-action = text-022.
          endif.
          if <ls_entityset>-action = 'R'.
            <ls_entityset>-action = text-023.
          endif.
           if <ls_entityset>-action = 'S'.
            <ls_entityset>-action = text-024.
          endif.
          <ls_entityset>-date = |{ <ls_entityset>-date+4(2) }/{ <ls_entityset>-date+6(2) }/{ <ls_entityset>-date+0(4) }|.
          <ls_entityset>-time = |{ <ls_entityset>-time+0(2) }:{ <ls_entityset>-time+2(2) }:{ <ls_entityset>-time+4(2) }|.
        endloop.
        sort et_entityset by date ascending time ascending.
      endif.
    endif.
  endmethod.

*    IT_NAVIGATION_PATH      =
**  importing
**    er_entity               =
**    es_response_context     =
*    .
**  catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception.
**  catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception.
**endtry.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->ITEMDETAILSSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TT_ITEMDETAILS
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method itemdetailsset_get_entityset.

    TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_data,
            prno type ebeln,
            pritem type ebelp,
            END OF ty_data.

    data: r_prno       type range of ebeln,
          ls_prno      like line of r_prno,
          R_prITEM     type range of ebelp,
          LS_prITEM    like line of R_prITEM,
          es_entityset like line of et_entityset,
          gt_data type TABLE of ty_data,
          gs_data type ty_data.

    data : lo_facade    type ref to /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_int_facade,
           lv_loginid   type string,
           lv_logintype type char1,
           lv_userrole  type char1,
           lv_bukrs     type bukrs,
           lv_lifnr     type lifnr,
           lv_level     type char10.

    lo_facade  ?= /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).

    zcl_supp_portal_utility=>read_header_parameter( exporting io_facade   = lo_facade
                                                    importing e_logintype = lv_logintype
                                                              e_loginid   = lv_loginid
                                                              e_userrole  = lv_userrole
                                                              e_ccode     = lv_bukrs ).
    if lv_loginid is not initial.
      select name from zrfq_user_master into @data(lv_InitiatorName) up to 1 rows
        where id = @lv_loginid
        order by name.
      endselect.
    endif.

    try.
        data(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PrNo' ].
        loop at ls_filter-select_options into data(ls_selopt).
          gs_data-prno = ls_selopt-low+0(10).
          gs_data-pritem = ls_selopt-low+10(5).
*          ls_prno-sign   = 'I'.
*          ls_prno-option = 'CP'.
          append gs_data to gt_data.

*          ls_prITEM-sign   = 'I'.
*          ls_prITEM-option = 'EQ'.
*          append ls_prITEM to R_prITEM.
        endloop.

*        data(ls_selopt) = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
*        ls_prno-low = ls_selopt-low.
*        ls_prno-sign   = 'I'.
*        ls_prno-option = 'CP'.
*        append ls_prno to r_prno.
*        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.



*    try.
*        ls_filter = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PrLineItem' ].
**        ls_selopt = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
*
*        loop at ls_filter-select_options into ls_selopt.
*        ls_prITEM-low = ls_selopt-low.
*        ls_prITEM-sign   = 'I'.
*        ls_prITEM-option = 'EQ'.
*        append ls_prITEM to R_prITEM.
**        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
*        ENDLOOP.
*      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
*    endtry.


    if gt_data is not initial.
      select * from zrfq_prdashboard into table @data(lt_data) FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @gt_data
        where prnumber = @gt_data-prno and pritem = @gt_data-pritem.
    endif.
*    select * from zrfq_prdashboard into table @data(lt_data) where prnumber in @r_prno and pritem in @r_pritem.
    if lt_data is not initial.
      select werks, bwkey from t001w into table @data(lt_t001w)
        for all entries in @lt_data
        where werks = @lt_data-plantcode.
      if lt_t001w is not initial.
        select bwkey, bukrs from t001k into table @data(lt_t001k)
          for all entries in @lt_t001w
          where bwkey = @lt_t001w-bwkey.
      endif.
    endif.

    loop at lt_data into data(ls_data).
      es_entityset-initiator = lv_loginid.
      es_entityset-initiatorname = lv_InitiatorName.
      es_entityset-prno = ls_data-prnumber.
      es_entityset-prcreationdate = ls_data-prdate.
      es_entityset-rerelesedate = ls_data-rel_date.
      es_entityset-plantcode = ls_data-plantcode.
      es_entityset-prlineitem = ls_data-pritem.
      es_entityset-matdes = ls_data-materialdescription.
      es_entityset-prquan = ls_data-prqty.
      es_entityset-prbudget = ls_data-totalprval.
      es_entityset-uom = ls_data-uom.
*      select bukrs from t001 into es_entityset-compcode where werks = ls_data-plantcode.
      read table lt_t001w into data(ls_t001w) with key werks = ls_data-plantcode.
      if sy-subrc is initial.
        read table lt_t001k into data(ls_t001k) with key bwkey = ls_t001w-bwkey.
        if sy-subrc is initial.
          es_entityset-compcode = ls_t001k-bukrs.
        endif.
      endif.
      es_entityset-deliverylocation = ls_data-plantaddress.
      append es_entityset to et_entityset.
      clear : es_entityset,ls_t001w,ls_t001k.
    endloop.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->NFAIDHELPSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TT_NFAIDHELP
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method nfaidhelpset_get_entityset.
    data : es_entityset type ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TS_NFAIDHELP.
    data : lo_facade    type ref to /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_int_facade,
           lv_loginid   type string,
           lv_logintype type char1,
           lv_userrole  type char1,
           lv_bukrs     type bukrs,
           lv_lifnr     type lifnr,
           lv_level     type char10,
            lv_top  type string,
          lv_skip type i,
           nfaid   type znfa_header-nfa_id.

    lo_facade  ?= /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).

    zcl_supp_portal_utility=>read_header_parameter( exporting io_facade   = lo_facade
                                                    importing e_logintype = lv_logintype
                                                              e_loginid   = lv_loginid
                                                              e_userrole  = lv_userrole
                                                              e_ccode     = lv_bukrs ).
*    IF lv_loginid is NOT INITIAL.
*    select nfaid from zdirect_nfa_head into table @data(lt_item) WHERE initiator = @lv_loginid.
*      ELSE.
*    select nfaid from zdirect_nfa_head into table @lt_item.
*    ENDIF.

*- Get number of records requested
    clear: lv_top,lv_skip.
    lv_top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).

**- Get number of lines that should be skipped
    lv_skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).
    lv_top = lv_top + lv_skip.
    nfaid =  iv_search_string.
    IF NFAID IS INITIAL.
    select nfaid from zdirect_nfa_head WHERE DRAFT is INITIAL  into table @data(lt_item) .
      ELSE.
          select nfaid from zdirect_nfa_head WHERE DRAFT is INITIAL AND nfaid = @NFAID  into table @lt_item .
            ENDIF .

*
    loop at lt_item into data(ls_item).
      es_entityset-nfaid = |{ ls_item-nfaid alpha = out }|.
      append es_entityset to et_entityset.
      clear es_entityset.
    endloop.
    SORT et_entityset by nfaid DESCENDING.

  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->QUERYSETSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TT_QUERYSET
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method querysetset_get_entityset.
    data : r_nfaid type range of znfa_header-nfa_id,
           l_nfaid like line of r_nfaid.

    try.
        data(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ].
        data(ls_selopt) = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
        l_NFAID-low = |{ ls_selopt-low alpha = in }|.
        l_NFAID-sign   = 'I'.
        l_NFAID-option = 'EQ'.
        append  l_NFAID to r_NFAID.
        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.

    select nfaid,
           eventid ,
           CONCAT( question, question1 ) as question,
           CONCAT( answer, answer1 ) as answer,
           querychk,
           raisedby,
           raisedon
       from zgnfa_query_tab
      where nfaid in @r_nfaid
      into corresponding fields of table @et_entityset.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->RECALLNFASET_CREATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_C(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TS_RECALLNFA
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method RECALLNFASET_CREATE_ENTITY.
DATA : LS_DATA TYPE ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>ts_recallnfa.

data(NFAID) = VALUE #( IT_KEY_TAB[ NAME = 'NFAID' ]-value OPTIONAL ).

IO_DATA_PROVIDER->read_entry_data(
  importing
    es_data = LS_DATA
).
*catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception. " mgw technical exception

DATA : lv_action TYPE zdnfa_hist-action,
       lv_remark TYPE zdnfa_hist-remark,
       lv_nfaid  TYPE zdnfa_hist-nfaid.
lv_action = 'RC'.
lv_remark = LS_DATA-remark.
lv_nfaid  = LS_DATA-NFAID.

UPDATE zdnfa_hist SET ACTION = @lv_action,
                         edate = @SY-DATLO,
                         etime  = @SY-uzeit,
                         remark = @lv_remark
WHERE NFAID = @lv_nfaid.
IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
COMMIT WORK.
ENDIF.
UPDATE zdirect_nfa_head SET draft = 'X',
                        status = 'D',
                        alevel = ' '
                        WHERE nfaid = @LS_DATA-NFAID.
IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
COMMIT WORK.
ENDIF.
  endmethod.

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->SEND_MAIL
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] I_APPROVER                     TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_REASON                       TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_BUYER                        TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_NFAID                        TYPE        CHAR10(optional)
* | [--->] I_EMAILTO                      TYPE        TT_EMAIL(optional)
* | [--->] I_EMAILCC                      TYPE        TT_EMAIL(optional)
* | [--->] I_SUBJECT                      TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_CONTENT                      TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [<---] RETURN                         TYPE        CHAR10
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
method send_mail.
  types: begin of ty_email,
           email type ad_smtpadr,
         end of ty_email.

  data: lt_email type table of ty_email,
        ls_email type ty_email.

  data: lt_mailrecipients type standard table of somlrec90,
        ls_mailrecipients type somlrec90,
        ls_mailsubject    type sodocchgi1,
        lt_packing_list   type standard table of sopcklsti1,
        ls_packing_list   type sopcklsti1,
        gv_cnt            type i,
        lv_partner        type bu_partner,
        lv_vendor         type lifnr,
        wa_spfli          type spfli.

  data: addsmtp   type table of bapiadsmtp,
        it_return type table of bapiret2.

  data:lv_month(2) type c,
       lv_year(4)  type c.
  data:lv_date type char10.
  data:lv_date1 type datum.

  data: lo_send_request  type ref to  cl_bcs,
        lo_document      type ref to  cl_document_bcs,
        lo_recipient     type ref to  if_recipient_bcs,
        lo_bcs_exception type ref to  cx_bcs,
        lt_mailtxt       type standard table of solisti1,
        l_mailtxt        type solisti1,
        sender1          type ref to cl_cam_address_bcs,
        lv_sent_to_all   type os_boolean,
        lv_subject       type char50,
        lv_nfaid         type char50.

  data: lo_sender        type ref to if_sender_bcs value is initial. " Added by shahrukh 08/01/2026.

  constants: lc_br  type string value '</br>' ##NO_TEXT,
             lc_br2 type string value '<br/>' ##NO_TEXT.
  if i_nfaid is not initial.
    lv_nfaid = |{ i_nfaid alpha = out }|.
  endif.
  case i_content.
    when 'MAIL1'.
      lt_mailtxt = value #(
        ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
        ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_approver },</br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = replace( val = text-025 sub = '&1' with = lv_nfaid ) && lc_br )
        ( line = text-026 && lc_br )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
 ( line = |<a href="https://azureportalprod-cqtz7hbe.launchpad.cfapps.in30.hana.ondemand.com/site?siteId=fb5c783b-a296-47c9-b4d3-a1a5d4285ace#Shell-home">Click here to view</a></br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-027 && lc_br2 )
        ( line = text-028 )
      ).
    when 'MAIL2'.
      lt_mailtxt = value #(
        ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
        ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = replace( val = text-029 sub = '&1' with = lv_nfaid ) && lc_br )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-030 && lc_br )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-031 )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-032 )
      ).
    when 'MAIL3'.
      lt_mailtxt = value #(
  ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
  ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
  ( line = |<br/>| )
  ( line = replace( val = text-033 sub = '&1' with = lv_nfaid ) && lc_br )
  ( line = text-034 && lc_br )
  ( line = |<br/>| )
  ( line = text-027 && lc_br2 )
  ( line = text-028 )
      ).
    when 'MAIL5'.
      lt_mailtxt = value #(
        ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
        ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = replace( val = replace( val = text-035 sub = '&1' with = i_nfaid ) sub = '&2' with = i_approver ) && lc_br )
        ( line = |{ i_reason }.</br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-027 && lc_br2 )
        ( line = text-028 )
      ).
                           when 'MAIL6'.
      lt_mailtxt = value #(
        ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
        ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = replace( val = text-036 sub = '&1' with = i_approver ) && lc_br )
        ( line = text-037 && lc_br )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-027 && lc_br2 )
        ( line = text-028 )
      ).
  endcase.

  try.
*     -------- create persistent send request ------------------------
      lo_send_request = cl_bcs=>create_persistent( ).

*     -------- create and set document -------------------------------
      lv_subject = i_subject.
      lo_document = cl_document_bcs=>create_document(
        i_type    = 'HTM'
        i_text    = lt_mailtxt
        i_subject = lv_subject ).


      data:lv_send type string.
      lv_send  = sy-uname.
      call method lo_send_request->set_document( lo_document ).

      try.
          lo_sender = cl_sapuser_bcs=>create( sy-uname ).
          lo_send_request->set_sender(
            exporting
              i_sender = lo_sender ).
        catch cx_address_bcs.
****Catch exception here
      endtry.
      """""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""EOC.


      loop at i_emailto into ls_email.
        if ls_email-email is not initial.
          lo_recipient = cl_cam_address_bcs=>create_internet_address(
            ls_email-email ).

*     add recipient with its respective attributes to send request
          call method lo_send_request->add_recipient
            exporting
              i_recipient = lo_recipient
              i_express   = 'X'.
          clear ls_email.
        endif.
      endloop.
      loop at i_emailcc into ls_email.
        if ls_email-email is not initial.
          lo_recipient = cl_cam_address_bcs=>create_internet_address(
            ls_email-email ).

*     add recipient with its respective attributes to send request
          call method lo_send_request->add_recipient
            exporting
              i_recipient = lo_recipient
              i_copy      = 'X'.
          clear ls_email.
        endif.
      endloop.

      call method lo_send_request->set_send_immediately
        exporting
          i_send_immediately = 'X'.

      call method lo_send_request->send(
        exporting
          i_with_error_screen = 'X'
        receiving
          result              = lv_sent_to_all ).
      if lv_sent_to_all is initial.
        return = 'X'.
      else.
        commit work and wait.
      endif.
    catch cx_bcs into lo_bcs_exception.
  endtry.
endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_DPC_EXT->VENDORHELPSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZDIRECT_NFA_PROCES_MPC=>TT_VENDORHELP
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method vendorhelpset_get_entityset.

    data: lv_top    type string,
          lv_skip   type i,
          r_lifnr   type range of lifnr,
          ls_lifnr  like line of r_lifnr,
          r_lifnr1  type range of lifnr,
          ls_lifnr1 like line of r_lifnr,
          r_name    type range of name1,
          ls_name   like line of r_name.

*    if iv_search_string is not initial.
*      lv_search_string = iv_search_string.
*      translate lv_search_string to upper case.
*      concatenate '*' lv_search_string '*' into lv_search_string.
*
*      ls_grp_Det-sign = 'I'.
*      ls_grp_Det-option = 'CP'.
*      translate lv_search_string to upper case.
*      ls_grp_Det-low = lv_search_string.
*      append ls_grp_Det to lt_grp_Det.
*      clear ls_grp_Det.
*
*    endif.

    try.
        data(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'SupplierRef' ].
        data(ls_selopt) = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
        ls_lifnr1-low = ls_selopt-low.
        ls_lifnr1-sign   = 'I'.
        ls_lifnr1-option = 'CP'.
        append ls_lifnr1 to r_lifnr1.
        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.

    try.
        ls_filter = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'VendorNo' ].
        ls_selopt = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
        ls_lifnr-low = ls_selopt-low.
        ls_lifnr-sign   = 'I'.
        ls_lifnr-option = 'CP'.
        append ls_lifnr to r_lifnr.
        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.

    try.
        ls_filter = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'VendorName' ].
        ls_selopt = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
        ls_name-low = ls_selopt-low.
        translate ls_name-low to upper case.
        ls_name-sign   = 'I'.
        ls_name-option = 'CP'.
        append ls_name to r_name.

        translate ls_name-low to lower case.
        append ls_name to r_name.

        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.
*    select a~lifnr,
**           ven_name,
*           b~name1,
*           a~status from zvend_onbrd  as a inner join lfa1 as b on a~lifnr = b~lifnr into table @et_entityset where  dummy_vendor in @r_lifnr or ven_name in @r_name .
*


    select dummy_vendor,
           lifnr,
           ven_name,
           status from zvend_onbrd into table @et_entityset
           where dummy_vendor <> @space or lifnr <> @space." where  dummy_vendor in @r_lifnr1 or
*            lifnr in @r_lifnr or ven_name in @r_name .

*    delete et_entityset where vendorno = ' '.


  endmethod.
ENDCLASS.
